It maintains comprehensive knowledge of all .md files and documented methods in the project.\n\nExamples:\n- &lt;example&gt;\n Context: User is implementing a new feature and wants to avoid duplicating existing functionality.\n user: "I need to add a function that validates user input"\n assistant: "Let me consult the project-docs-analyzer ag… ([mosgaragedev/Agentic-Dev-Env](https://github.com/mosgaragedev/Agentic-Dev-Env/blob/HEAD/claude-dev/packages/claude-dev-env/agents/project-docs-analyzer.md) · 2 copies)
- **project-docs-auditor**: Use this agent to audit out-of-code documentation (README, CLAUDE.md, CONTRIBUTING.md, configuration files) for accuracy against the actual codebase. Unlike the documentation-auditor (which covers in-code docstrings and comments), this agent focuses on external-facing documentation. It has four concrete, verifiable capabilities — reference validation (do things mentioned in docs exist in code?, backed by `check_doc_refs.py`), cross-file consistency (do documentation files agree with each other and with project metadata?), structural completeness (does the project document what it actually exp… ([ReviewToolkits/code-review-toolkit](https://github.com/ReviewToolkits/code-review-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-review-toolkit/agents/project-docs-auditor.md) · ★6)

- **project-docs-manager**: 当需要管理项目文档时使用此代理，包括在开发开始前查询现有文档、开发完成后更新文档，或维护文档结构和最佳实践。示例：&lt;example&gt;上下文：用户即将开始开发新功能，需要先检查现有文档。用户：'我要实现用户身份验证' 助手：'让我先咨询项目文档管理器代理来检查现有文档并了解当前进度，然后再开始开发。' &lt;commentary&gt;由于用户正在开始开发工作，应首先使用项目文档管理器代理查询现有文档和进度。&lt;/commentary&gt;&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;上下文：用户完成了功能实现，需要更新文档。用户：'我已完成支付网关集成的实现' 助手：'现在我将使用项目文档管理器代理来更新项目文档中的新支付网关实现。' &lt;commentary&gt;由于开发已完成，使用项目文档管理器代理相应地更新文档。&lt;/commentary&gt;&lt;/example&gt; ([laofahai/linch-mind-deprecated](https://github.com/laofahai/linch-mind-deprecated/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/project-docs-manager.md) · ★2)
